HFT visibility is governed by the authoring policy above.","docket_mode":"blocked","docket_ready":false}},"selected_context_units":[],"support_registry":[{"branch_refs":["root_000291/B006","root_000335/B001","root_000357/B002","root_001409/B001","root_001410/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:3","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_1ba0573cda35794ef40f","text":"3. **Inward bearing is inverted into destructive issue.** Branch evidence: `ح م ل` B002, pregnancy and inward bearing; `ر ح م` B003, womb; `ح ب ل` B006, pregnancy extending in the womb; `م ر ء` B001, woman; `ح ط ب` B001, fuel. Structural cue: a woman is named as a `حَمَّالَة`, while the packet opens with womb/kinship mercy and ends with a rope whose branches include pregnancy. Assigned role: carrying bridges fertility imagery and fuel imagery. Abductive move: I infer an inversion: the female capacity to bear inward life is overlaid by bearing outward fuel. Reading change: the phrase becomes an anti-gestation image, where what is borne issues in combustion rather than mercy or life.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000335/B003","root_000357/B003"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-2","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_1e8fda05494326171fa3","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"111:4:2\",\"root_000357\",[\"B003\"]],[\"111:4:3\",\"root_000335\",[\"B003\"]]],\"grade\":\"strong\",\"text\":\"Odun, zarar verici söz ve dedikodu olarak da işler: taşıdığı şey düşmanlığı dışarıda körüklerken sorumluluğu kendi üzerine yükler.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000357/B003","root_000357/B004"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-4","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_3280f4da5151a91ca18a","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"111:4:2\",\"root_000357\",[\"B003\",\"B004\"]]],\"grade\":\"weak\",\"text\":\"Malın güvence olamadığı yerde onun taşıması, borcu başkasından uzaklaştıran bir kefalet değil, paylaşılan ve devredilemeyen bir sorumluluk olur.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000357/B007"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-6","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_3c6a9b5a8cf78d9583c9","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"111:4:2\",\"root_000357\",[\"B007\"]]],\"grade\":\"weak\",\"text\":\"Son ipin süreklilik çağrışımı, taşıyıcılığı tek seferlik bir işten çıkarıp tekrarlanan, yorucu bir uğraşa dönüştürür.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:retrospective_surprises_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_3ed31448a603775ccb81","text":"`111:5` directly realizes the rope already embedded in `ح ط ب` branch `B002`'s night-gatherer image; its placement on her neck turns the gathering apparatus back upon the gatherer. The final `مَسَد` branch `B003` also adds duration, making `حَمَّالَة` sustained exertion rather than a static title. A weaker but newly visible bodily relation comes from root `م ر ء` branch `B004`: its `root_001409` variant is the esophagus near the throat, while its `root_001410` variant concerns eating or occasion-feeding; the later neck image and the carried fuel together invert feeding into supplying what the fire consumes.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000291/B002","root_000357/B003","root_000357/B004","root_001110/B002","root_001457/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:4","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_6224a11bc1c7fce48bd5","text":"4. **She bears liability rather than merely objects.** Branch evidence: `ح م ل` B003, bearing responsibility/sin; `ح م ل` B004, guarantee/indemnity; earlier `غ ن ي` B002, availing/replacing; `م و ل` B001, property; later `ح ب ل` B002, covenant/protection. Structural cue: wealth failed to avail in 111:2, and now a household member bears. Assigned role: carrying is moral/legal load-bearing, not just transport. Abductive move: I infer that after the failure of money as guarantee, the human carrier embodies the burden that cannot be transferred away. Reading change: she appears as co-bearer of obligation and harm, not as an incidental prop.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000335/B001","root_000357/B001","root_001379/B001","root_001409/B001","root_001410/B001","root_001564/B002"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_68c0b12600f971dc92c7","text":"1. **The wife is an active fuel-supplier in the same mechanism as the fire.** Branch evidence: `م ر ء` B001, woman/person; `ح م ل` B001, bearing an outward load; `ح ط ب` B001, firewood gathered as fuel; earlier `ن و ر` B002, burning fire; `ل ه ب` B001, blazing flame. Structural cue: the fire of 111:3 is immediately followed by the woman carrying fuel. Assigned role: she supplies the logistical link between fire and combustible material. Abductive move: I infer that juxtaposition makes her carrying feed the preceding fire. Reading change: she is not a peripheral spouse; she becomes part of a supply chain that sustains the blaze.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000357/B001","root_000357/B002","root_001422/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:retrospective_surprises_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_8336a8db9f893ecf9b20","text":"- 111:5 immediately changed the wife-carrier image: the rope in her neck means the carrier is also carried, led, or constrained. `ح م ل` B001 and `ح ب ل` B001 therefore form a loop rather than a one-way action. - The final `مَسَد` strengthened the material register of `حَطَب`. Twisting (`م س د` B001), binding (`ح ب ل` B001), and fuel (`ح ط ب` B001) make a coherent workshop of punishment: gathering, carrying, tying, and burning. - The pregnancy branches were more surprising after the final ayah than during first reading. `ح م ل` B002, `ح ب ل` B006, and the opening `ر ح م` B003 create an anti-gestational counterimage: a woman bears not life but fuel/liability, and the cord is not hidden in the womb but exposed at the neck.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:4","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_8ad986ef91e475b32883","text":"4. **Familial bearing is inverted from generation to propagation of harm.** **Lexical evidence:** dormant root `ح م ل` branch `B002` covers pregnancy and inward fruit-bearing; `ٱمْرَأَتُهُ`, root `م ر ء`, branch `B001`, supplies the woman within a marriage relation. The opening root `ر ح م` branches `B002` (kinship) and `B003` (womb), and the final root `ح ب ل` branch `B006` (pregnancy extending in the womb), reinforce this generative field. **Structure:** instead of an inward life-bearing load, the intensive carrier bears dry fuel outward toward flame. **Role and abduction:** the packet supplies woman, kinship, womb, pregnancy, and outward fuel-bearing; I infer an inversion in which household generativity propagates combustion rather than life. **Reading change:** `حَمَّالَة` holds a dark counter-image to nurturing fertility: repeated bearing that multiplies destructive material.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000335/B002","root_000357/B006"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-5","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_8f7265cd51a4aa3b5bfa","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"111:4:2\",\"root_000357\",[\"B006\"]],[\"111:4:3\",\"root_000335\",[\"B002\"]]],\"grade\":\"weak\",\"text\":\"Toplama ve taşıma aracı, sonraki boyun bağıyla taşıyanın kendisini yakalayan bir düzene dönüşür.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000335/B002","root_000335/B003","root_001564/B007"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:2","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_94d4e392f571ac7d4e9b","text":"2. **Carrying firewood also reads as carrying destructive speech.** Branch evidence: `ح ط ب` B003, carrying wood as tale-bearing and stirring harm; `ح ط ب` B002, night wood-gatherer mixing bad and good; earlier `ن و ر` B007, enmity flaring between people; `ك س ب` B001/B002, gain for self or brought to another. Structural cue: after wealth/earning and fire, the carrier appears with a noun whose branch explicitly includes slander and social ignition. Assigned role: firewood becomes speech-material that inflames relations. Abductive move: I supply that the carried object may be both literal fuel and socially combustible report. Reading change: the ayah reads as a portrait of participation in hostile circulation, not only physical labor.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:2","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_b117ab11a60193699795","text":"2. **Speech-harm becomes a borne moral liability.** **Lexical evidence:** root `ح ط ب`, branch `B003`, treats carrying wood as tale-bearing, slander, and kindling harm; root `ح م ل`, branch `B003`, is responsibility, sin, trust, or moral burden. The preceding `نَار`, root `ن و ر`, branch `B007`, can denote enmity flaring among people. **Structure:** person (`ٱمْرَأَتُهُ`), intensive role (`حَمَّالَة`), and carried object (`ٱلْحَطَب`) form a complete agent-action-load construction after the social-fire possibility in `111:3`. **Role and abduction:** the packet supplies tale-bearing, burden, and enmity; I infer that carried reports cause hostility and return as the carrier's own liability. **Reading change:** the phrase diagnoses a social causal loop: she bears harmful speech outward and bears its responsibility inward.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_cb7c21ccbbcbd6449282","text":"1. **An agent who supplies the preceding blaze.** **Lexical evidence:** `حَمَّالَة` has root `ح م ل`, branch `B001` (bearing an outward load), and `ٱلْحَطَب` has root `ح ط ب`, branch `B001` (firewood gathered, carried, or brought to another as fuel). `111:3` supplies `ص ل ي` branch `B004` (kindling and fuel) and `ل ه ب` branch `B001` (ignition). **Structure:** the conjunction introduces the wife directly after the man's fire sentence, and the intensive carrier noun is completed by the exact material that can sustain it. **Role and abduction:** load, fuel, and blaze are supplied; I infer that her repeated carrying enables the preceding fire's continuation. **Reading change:** the wife is not a passive relational appendage: the ayah assigns her an operational role in the fire mechanism.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000335/B001","root_000357/B001"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-1","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_eaa5491f954d817d0bef","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"111:4:2\",\"root_000357\",[\"B001\"]],[\"111:4:3\",\"root_000335\",[\"B001\"]]],\"grade\":\"strong\",\"text\":\"Kadın, önceki alevin kenarında duran bir eş değil, taşıdığı odunla onu besleyen etkin tedarikçi olarak görünür.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:3","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_ee22402ea027fb3ab30e","text":"3. **The collection tool rebounds onto the collector.** **Lexical evidence:** root `ح ط ب`, branch `B002`, images the night wood-gatherer who indiscriminately collects good and bad in a rope and may be harmed by it; `111:5` immediately supplies that `حَبْل`, root `ح ب ل`, branches `B001` (binding cord) and `B005` (entangling snare). Root `ح م ل`, branch `B006`, adds carrying gear or a strap. **Structure:** carrier and gathered wood are followed without an intervening scene by a cord on the carrier's own neck. **Role and abduction:** the packet itself supplies collection-in-a-rope and possible recoil; I infer that the same apparatus used to aggregate and transport the load becomes the means of restraint. **Reading change:** `حَمَّالَةَ ٱلْحَطَب` becomes a self-ensnaring practice, not just an occupational label.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"anchor_evidence":[],"anchor_evidence_coverage":{"boundary":"Exact Arabic verifies surface contact only.
